tbmm-2021-12-12sit01spe0002par0434	Our Ministry of National Education is mobilising for everyone to reach education on equal terms, to gain the knowledge, skill, attitude, and behavior required by the age, to grow up as entrepreneurs, innovative, language skills high, communication and learning, self-confidence and responsibility, healthy and happy individuals. One of the most affected areas in the world by the global epidemic has been education. Our educational system is going through a dynamic, continuous process of making new decisions. In addition to the plague, the point of remote education in Turkey is a single example of the world in terms of quality and spread. The past school year has been based on the principle of continuing the study, which is being conducted remotely, depending on the increase of the number of cases. The Ministry of National Education saw not only as educational, but as a social improvement and normalization in a sense. At the point where we're here today, the basic philosophy is that schools are considered to be the institutions that must first be opened and closed. As you will agree, our schools have made significant contributions to the normalisation process, which has been taken into account and kept open. Consider: While many countries have trouble producing mask supplies and disinfectant, they have produced medical masks and disinfectant for both health workers and citizens in our country. Along with the good examples in many of our provinces, my science was also made by Karatay Kılıçaslan Occupational and Technical Anatolian High School to build a mask-making machine at its own expense. Today, these machines produce 80,000 masks daily, this year's turnover is 8 million. Again, Selçuk Mehmet Tuza Pakpen gives his student disinfectant and controls it by measuring his temperature and transmitting information to the system through a device produced by his vocational and Technical Anadolu High School. Our same school makes 250 kinds of mechanical parts with mass production in the defense industry. Our schools have been a source of both material and moral support for our country with a common consciousness and understanding beyond being a passive institution during this epidemic. I'd like to congratulate you on every one of our student and teacher friends. I'd like to pass on a few improvements in my time. In terms of social development, any profession is important. But the place and the importance of teaching is different because teachers pass on our social culture and values as they are the growers of every profession. Therefore, the work of healing for the teaching profession has always been appreciated. In addition to the 3,600 additional indicators promised to our teacher, the teacher's profession law, which Mr. President gave the good news on Teacher's Day, has pleased our teachers with their professional careers and additional financial and social rights. Our country has also achieved promising improvements in education in both national and international evaluation criteria. The figures found in 2018 PISA and TIMS 2019 are not lying. Our country's standard of education has been confirmed by international independent organisations and included in independent researchers' reports. It is important that the EC PARTY budget always puts education first.
